| AI-73 | **A ship's landing craft is a vehicle of its own, and the boat helm regulates its speed.** Every vanilla ship template carries its craft as child `ObjectSpawner`s (`HatsuzukiDaihatsuSpawner`, `ShokakuDaiHatsuSpawner`, `FletcherLcvpSpawner`, `Enterprise_lcvpSpawner`, `GatoDaiHatsuSpawner`, `Sub7CDaiHatsuSpawner`, `YamatoDaihatsuSpawner`, `PrinceOWLcvpSpawner` in `Objects.rfa`); the engine spawns each as its own object. `BoatControl::towardsDirection` 0x0860df70 is `actionStatusDecision` then `speedControl` 0x0860cf40: past 30 deg in state 0 it turns (full rudder `sign(v) sign(dir . right)`, throttle `-sign(v)` above 3 m/s, else a full throttle kept or `sign(v)`); within 30 deg the wanted speed is the Mobile plug-in's `maxSpeed` (+8) x a factor from `getLevel(map, pos)` 0x0847ca60 (the free quadtree block's level) against the map's base level (+0xc4a4) and the angle (level > base+1: 1 / 0.8 past 30 / 0.6 past 50 deg; base+1: 1 to 15 / 0.6 / 0.4 past 50; else 0.3 past 30, 0.5 from 2 to 15, 0.4), the throttle `simpleReg` 0x08613c20 = clamp(wanted - last tick's speed (+0x54)), the rudder `sign(x) log10(9 abs(x) + 1)` with `x = sin(angle) - 0.1 yawRate`. `EntryBoatMoveTo::execute` 0x08613d60 passes no speed cap (1e9) and calls `resetControls` inside the move's radius. | **read** (2026-09-23) | Built: the craft is moved under `spawners` at load (seats.js `detachSpawnedCraft`), `boatSpeedControl` and the turn branch, `freeLevel` for `getLevel` (base level 2, AI-66 INFERRED). Two viewer bugs found on the way: the helm braked at every look-ahead point (the move's 4 x radius arrival applied to it), and `hullGeometry`'s no-chain fallback took a world AABB (a yawed Daihatsu read 20.8 m square with a 3 m keel and grounded in 2.7 m of water). Live, Wake: a bot takes the Daihatsu off the Hatsuzuki as its own unit, the `LandingCraft3` map builds in ~0.5 s (93% of the level open), and the craft runs 558 m to a beach point on the south shore (886, -938), regulated at 15.1 m/s (its `maxSpeed` 15.5), and beaches 23 m past it at (891, -915). Not built: `actionStatusDecision` states 1..9, `resetControls`' braking on arrival, the SAI's landing-zone orders (`isLandingZoneUser`, `WPBeachLanding`). |
| AI-74 | **Five review findings read against the binary.** (1) `StrategyPrerequisite::evaluate` 0x0863aa50 sums `condition x weight` over its conditions (abort conditions skipped unless the strategy is active); strength 0/1 (Required / RequiredPositive, `operator<<` 0x08486de0) returns 0 below 0 and otherwise **adds** (0x0863aafd), 2 (RequiredNegative) returns 0 above 0 and adds, 4 / 5 (Advisory Positive / Negative) clip to 0 and add, the rest add. (2) `AIStrategicArea::update` 0x0863d6d0: an area whose control-point object (+0x138) is -1 is set Owned (0) for a side with units there and none of the enemy's when that side may take it (+0x15f + side), Hostile (1) when only enemy units are there and the enemy may take it (+0x160 + (side == 1)), else unchanged; one with a control point follows its team. (3) `BBFire::calculateUrgency` 0x08563570 carries one factor (`fStack_1a4`): 0.75 when the bot is outside its ordered area (`isInside` at 0x085639f3), x0.75 when a target is outside it (0x08564015), replaced by 0.5 in range; it multiplies each strength term and the score, so it counts twice. (4) The SAI period is `AISettings` +0x28 = 2.0 (`reset` 0x0848461a; `getSAIUpdateFrequency` is vt+0x44, read by `SAI::update` 0x086306d0 against +0x1d8). (5) The stall counts (150 / 401) are 30 Hz AI ticks. | **read** (2026-09-23) | Built: the prerequisite sum, presence ownership, F in `scoreTargets`, `SAI.updateFrequency` 2.0, the stall count advancing by `dt x 30`, and the left-vehicle ramp keyed by the hull (it compared a seat id with a hull id). Pinned in `tests/test_strategic.py` and `tests/test_bot_ai.py`. |
| AI-75 | **A side's knowledge of an enemy decays over the template's `degeneration`.** Every AI object holds one `Information` per side (`AIObjectReal` +0x2c + side x 4). Its own side's is an `InformationReal` (`getSecurity` 0x085e8d00 / 0x085e8d10 = `fld1`); a template with `commonKnowledge 1` makes an `AIObjectShared` whose one `InformationReal` serves everybody (`AITemplate::createObject` 0x085dd500 on +0x18, `AIObjectShared::createInformation` 0x085db040). Any other side's is an `InformationKnown` made on demand: `AIObjectReal::getInformation(side)` 0x085d8970 calls `createInformation(t, side)` 0x085d8ca0 when the slot is empty, which builds it from the template (`AIObjectReal` +0x38) and ends in `setTime(t)`. `BotMain::sense` 0x08521cf0 asks for every object `getWorldFrustumObjects` (env vt+0x50) returns in the band's frustum, and for an occupied unit's occupier and each secondary's occupier, **before any ray**: an enemy enters the side's grid at security 1 the first time it stands in any bot's frustum band, seen or not; asking again neither makes nor refreshes. The ctor 0x085e7f50 stores +0x3c = t, +0x40 = template +0xc, +8 = template +0x10, +0x14/+0x18 = template +0x14, +4 = template +8; the `aiTemplate.*` console handlers (`executeObjectMethod` 0x084ff260 / 0x084ff660 / 0x084ffa60 / 0x084ffe60 / 0x08500260 / 0x08500670) name them: +8 `types` (`addType`), +0xc **`degeneration`**, +0x10 `allowedTimeDiff` (the position refresh gate in `setTime(t)` 0x085e8050: +8 + last refresh < t), +0x14 `basicTemp`, +0x18 `commonKnowledge`, +0x19 `secondary`. `getSecurity(t)` 0x085e8670 = 1 - SCurve((t - +0x3c) / +0x40); `getSecurity()` 0x085e86a0 at the AI timer's now. Refreshes: `setTime(t)` sets +0x3c = t; `event_SpottedEnemyObject` 0x08523ae0 calls it on the spotted object's information and on every secondary's (vt+0x80 / vt+0x78, the other seats of the hull); `updateMemory` 0x085244e0 on a remembered object each time a re-test sees it (0x08524b05); `event_soundEmitter` 0x085237c0 and `updateHearingMemory` 0x085240e0 call `setTime(t, 0.5)` 0x085e8210, which clamps q to 0..1 and, only when the current security is below q, sets +0x1c = -1 and calls `setTime(t - 1 / (q x +0x40))` (`de f1` = `fdivrp`, 1 / (q D), checked against the bytes; q = 0 sets +0x3c = 0). `SCurve::calculate` 0x08658420: 1 from 1, 0 below 0, else the 101-entry `SCurve::init` 0x08658030 table blended at `trunc(100 x)` (the control word is set to truncate at 0x08658457; Ghidra shows ROUND). `SAI::updateStrengths` 0x08636c20 walks its own side's grid (env vt+0xdc) with a `UnitPredicate` and weighs each occupied unit by that information's vt+0x18. Values (`Objects.rfa`, vanilla): the soldier `degeneration 15` (`allowedTimeDiff 0.5`); planes 5 (Aichi Val 8, B-17 7); jeeps and bikes 10; Sherman, Panzer IV, T-34-85, Chi-Ha, Hanomag, Priest, Wespe, Katyusha 15; Tiger, T-34, M10, Sexton 25; M3A1, landing craft, AA and fixed guns 20; Defgun 60; destroyers 50; battleships 90; carriers 180; buildings 1 with `commonKnowledge 1`. | **read** (2026-09-24) | Built: bot-sense.js `SideKnowledge` (contact before the rays, spot + crewmates, re-sight, hearing), `sCurveExact` on the read table, bot-strength.js `EnemyStrengthTables.update(units, now)` weighing each enemy by its side's security (a unit never contacted is not summed; a dead player is forgotten) and `VEHICLE_DEGENERATION`; the referee passes player ids and the clock. Pinned in `tests/test_bot_ai.py`. Sim, El Alamein seed 1: nobody is known until the sides meet at 77 s; a tank contacted then fades to security 0 by 93 s and the Allies' `HeavyArmour` type count from 0.82 to 0.06 without a new sighting. INVENTION: knowledge keyed by player, not AI object; a mod vehicle takes the soldier's 15. Not wired: the Fire scoring's security (`scoreVehicleTargets` still 1) and the sensing quadrants' heard `security 1` (bot-decision.js). |
| AI-76 | **A hull's crew dies to whoever dealt the lethal hit; a burn-down has no killer.** | **confirmed** (2026-09-24) | `GameServer::_giveDamage` (lnxded 0x0814b870) resolves the attacker id (`getBFPlayer`, 0x0814b92c) and only then sets the Armor's last hitter through vtable +0x74 (0x0814b947; `Armor+0x14`, the id `Armor::status` 0x081739e0 resolves for `addVehicleDestroyed`). For a PCO it computes `lethal = hp - damage <= 0.001` and walks the hull's seats (`getPcos`, 0x0814baea); for each living occupant on the lethal call: with an attacker of the other side, `handleScore(attacker, 3, victim)` then `killPlayer(victim, true)` (0x0814c122 / 0x0814c12f and 0x0814c277 / 0x0814c284); his own side, a team kill 6 (0x0814c15b / 0x0814c2d0, only when the call's last bool argument is set) then `killPlayer(victim, false)`; no attacker, `killPlayer(victim, false)` (0x0814c224, 0x0814c399), which `killPlayer` 0x0814d920 sends as score 4 (`is no more`) where the killed case sends 5. The critical burn in `Armor::update` (0x08172f40) damages through `IGameServer::giveDamage` (GameServer secondary vptr +0xf4 = the thunk 0x08159960) with attacker -1 (0x0817322a), so a hull that burns out after a hit kills its crew with no killer; `GameServer::destroyObject` 0x08131a20 likewise kills anyone left seated with `killPlayer(p, false)`. `Armor::update` also expires the last hitter: when its countdown +0x2c runs out it writes -1 to +0x14. Client line (score event 3, `FUN_004933d0` case 3, 0x00494342..0x00494419): `killer + L" [" (0x008d89a4) + word + L"] " (0x008d96b0) + victim`, the word the lexicon's `DEFAULT_KILL_TEXT` unless the stamped template id resolves (template manager `DAT_0097d768` vtable +0x1c). Built: `vehicle-damage.js` `lastHitPlayer` / `killedBy` (the lethal call's attacker), fed by `vehicle-hits.js` (both round paths) and the referee's `damageHull`; `bot-units.js attackerOf`; a human killed in his hull through `vehicle-wrecks.js` `noteHullKiller`; `chat-log.js deathLines`. Not built: the last hitter's expiry (+0x2c) and the team-kill gate (the viewer prints the team kill whenever the teams match) |
| AI-77 | **`WPCloseTo`: the engine's waypoint on an object, and it is never built.** `WPCloseTo(objectId, radius)` ctor 0x085365b0 / 0x08536780: +8 the object id, +0xc R = max(5, radius), +0x10 = 25.0, +0x14..+0x1c the stored goal; for an object with a physical body (`informationManager` entry +0x24) R = max(R, 2 x its vt+0x30, read as the bounding radius: INFERRED) and the goal is the object's centre, its position (vt+0x1c) plus its matrix (vt+0x28) times the body's centre offset (vt+0x2c). `getUrgency` 0x08536990: the goal is the object's centre now, d^2 its 3D distance squared from the bot (stored at +0x10); d^2 < R^2 returns 0 and sets +4 (0x08536b28); else u = min(1, d^2 / 4R^2) (the clamp at 0x08536af5, no 0.1 floor, no x2), and when the goal is more than R from the stored one it is re-stored and +4 set (0x08536b91); a vanished object sets +5 and returns 0. `getGoalPoint` 0x08536ce0 returns the stored goal; `getWayPointRadius` 0x08536d40 R; `getId` 0x08536de0 0. `modifyMaxSpeed` 0x08536bf0 (compares checked in the disassembly): a mobile object (info +0x10 bit 2) gives min(own, the object's speed vt+0x1c on +0x2c) while d^2 <= R -- R, not R^2, as compiled -- else the own speed (the 1.33 x d^2 / R branch sits under R < d^2 and needs 2 d^2 <= R: unreachable); a fixed object stops the follower there. No caller of either ctor and no other write of its vtable 0x0875d748 in `bf1942_lnxded.static`: no retail bot ever holds one. `WPAltitudeCloseTo` (ctors 0x085350c0 / 0x08535100) is the same, likewise never built. | **read** (2026-09-24) | Built as the law of the doctrine layer's follow and hold orders (doctrine.js `closeToOrder`, `WPFollow` / `WPHold`): R, the urgency, the re-store past R (a new order object, since the viewer re-plans on identity). Not built: `modifyMaxSpeed` (the viewer's plans carry no speed cap). Pinned in `tests/test_doctrine.py`. The squad play that uses it is INVENTION (features/bot-doctrines). |

## Netcode (2026-09-20; the client send-batch cap, the ghost apply step, one ClientConnection ctor, and the register→wire send path stay open)

The engine's multiplayer architecture, read for
[`features/netcode-play-multiplayer/`](../netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md)
(P0 research; P2's wire format): an authoritative server stepping the same
fixed 30 Hz simulation the client runs, consuming one buffered `PlayerAction`
per tick per player, with the client predicting its own player and remote
entities ghost-overwritten at 0.1 s. Re-derived by an independent verifier
(verdict 2026-09-20, binding); narrative:
[subsystems/netcode.md](subsystems/netcode.md). All addresses
`bf1942_lnxded.static` unless marked client. The verifier's own caveats carry
through: NEEDS-MORE rows stay open, and nothing here was inferred from the
first reader's text.

| # | Finding | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| W-1 | The wire action record is **`PlayerAction`**: six 12-bit channels over bytes 0–11 in fixed order **Yaw, Pitch, Roll, Throttle, MouseLookX, MouseLookY**, then a **u32 button mask at byte 12** — 104 bits; byte 16 is never serialized | **verified** | `writeToStream` `0x08148750` / `readFromStream` `0x081486f0`: six `(write\|read)Unsigned(stream, 12)` into/from a `short[0..5]`, then 32 bits at `+0xc`. Order from `PlayerAction::set` `0x081128a0` (channel 0..5 → action channel 0..5, mask bit i gates input channel i) and the `c_PIYaw … c_PIMouseLookY` rodata at `0x086c86a5`. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-2 | Encode is **`floatToFixed(v, 12, 16.0)`**: `v/16` clamped to ±1, then 12-bit — `round(4095·(clamp(v/16, −1, 1) + 1)/2)` | **verified** | `floatToFixed` `0x08113480` (`fdivrp` by 16.0, `fucom` clamps via `test ah,0x45`, `(1<<bits)−1 · (v/16+1) · 0.5`); constants as bytes: −1.0f `0x86b05ec`, 0.5 `0x86be450`, 16.0f `0x41800000` at every `set` channel. The same ±16 headroom GUN-2b warns about. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-3 | Decode is **`((2n/4095) − 1)·16` rounded to 0.01**, gated per channel on `isInputMapped` | **verified** | `PlayerAction::get` `0x0815c5a0`: `n/4095.0` (`0x86c0cf0`), ×2, −1, ×16.0 (`0x86c0cf8`), ×100 (`0x86b01ac`), `frndint`, ÷100. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-4 | **ch48 (Communication) never crosses the wire** — receivers reconstruct it as 0 from an initialized-zero `PlayerAction` | **verified** | `get` reads ch48 from byte 16 (`param_1+8` dword bit 0x100); `writeToStream`/`readFromStream` only cover bytes 0–15. [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| W-5 | **`operator<<`/`>>` (`0x081d89f0`/`0x081d8bc0`) are console name-index helpers, NOT the wire format** — the design README's "`>>` operator is the spec for our wire format" is overturned | **verified** (corrects [`features/netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md`](../netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md)) | `operator<<` is a 56-case `switch` over channel names in rodata (`0x086c86a5..`, jump table `0x086c88c4`), no serialization of any kind; `operator>>` reads a `std::string` and `compare`s it against the same names. The wire spec is `PlayerAction` (W-1…W-3). [netcode.md §1](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-1 | The server's per-frame order is **processLocalPlayersInputs → processReceivedPackets → one simulateFrame per tick → updateStateMasks/updateVoteSystem/updateBanList → updateConnections → processGameStateAndSendPackets (the send) → clearPlayerActions** | **verified** | `GameServer::update` `0x08132940`; the per-tick call is GameServer vtable `0x0871b0e0`+8 slot **+0x140** = `simulateFrame` `0x0815c2a0`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-2 | Each remote player's buffered actions are trimmed to **≤ 4, dropping the OLDEST**; a trim also writes `player+0x174 = 0` | **verified** | `clearPlayerActions` `0x0815bb90`: per connection, player's ActionBuffer at `BFPlayer+0x144`; `while (4 < size)` pop the oldest. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-3 | **Exactly one** action is consumed per player per tick | **verified** | `GameServer::simulatePlayerUpdate` `0x0815bd00` reads the FIRST ActionBuffer entry and erases it; `simulatePlayersUpdate` `0x0815bfa0` calls it once per player per tick (remote connections); sets `player+0x14b`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-4 | Empty buffer → **zeroed input**; the receive side **dedupes by serial** (drops `≤ last`); a backlog **> 9 pending ticks collapses to 1** (both binaries) | **verified** (all three) | Empty: `0x0815bd00` decodes the sentinel and runs an explicit 55-float zero loop. Dedupe: `processRcvdPlayerActions` `0x08148470` pushBack only when the entry's serial (`+0x88`) is `> lastSerial` (`+0x68`); serial signed 32-bit, count field 4-bit in `processReceivedPacket` `0x081484e0`. Backlog: server `0x08132940` (`9 < n → n = 1`); client `GameClient::update` `0x0048fca0` `cmp ebx,0x9; jle` at `0x48fca8`–`0x48fcb1` (bridge had lost this function — re-derived from PE bytes). [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-5 | Client routing is `Setup::dispatchPlayerInput` → `Game::addPlayerInput`, a **`map<int, list<PlayerInput>>` insert** per tick | **verified** | `0x00448520` ends in `Game__addPlayerInput` `0x0040ecb0` (PE bytes): map find/insert on `Game+0x2c` (`0x0063c8a0`), `list::push_back` (`0x0040e270`), `ret 0x8`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| D-6 | Client consume: **one ActionBuffer pop per local player per tick** — pack via `PlayerAction::set`, then get → `handleInput` → `performHandleUpdate` | **verified** | `GameClient::processLocalPlayersInputs` `0x00488840` (live); `GameClient::simulatePlayerUpdate` `0x004b6a30` (live): pops one `PlayerAction::get` (`0x004913a0`) entry, erase, `handleInput` vtable+0x30, `performHandleUpdate` `0x004b7680`. [netcode.md §2](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-1 | The server join handshake is **`receive()==1` → new ClientConnection → ServerInfoEvent/ServerInfoEvent2 → ChallengeEvent → chokeBandwidth**; the ctor fixes **20 Hz send rate (conn+0xc), 1044-byte budget (conn+0x10), 1024-byte send `BitStream(0x400)`** | **verified** | `processReceivedPackets` `0x08137ba0`; ClientConnection ctor `0x081142a0` (`param_1[3]` = raw bits `0x14` = 20, `param_1[4]` = raw bits `0x414` = 1044). The second BitStream ctor there is invoked with no explicit arg — its default limit stays **open**. [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-2 | **IService / IJoinService / IHostService are empty stubs with no handshake** — every vtable slot is the same single import stub; the design README's "joiners connect to a dedicated host service" must not be built on them | **verified** (corrects [`features/netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md`](../netcode-play-multiplayer/README.md)) | Client PE: `0x4010b0`/`0x4010e0`/`0x401120` load vtables `0x8c37d8`/`0x8c37e0`/`0x8c3810` (plus adjacent copies `0x4010c0`/`0x4010f0`/`0x401130`/`0x401140`); all three vtables' slots = `0x00804ae0` = `jmp ds:0x8c352c`. The real handshake is J-1's server flow. [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-3 | Disconnect is an **explicit packet type 0xd, then teardown** | **verified** | `GameClient::disconnect` `0x00490f00` (PE bytes, full body): `push 0xd; call [vtable+0x18]` at `0x490f47`, then `+0x88` cleared, state `+0x8`/`+0xc` set to 4, `+0x34` nulled, held objects released. [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| J-4 | The bf42plus netcode patches are failure-mode history for the above | **verified** | `0x00490f4c` (inside disconnect; `GameClient_disconnect_udp` force-disconnect point), `0x00495fd2` (`force_disconnect_msg` via `0x6a7ee0`), `0x006d8461` (`skip_spawn_screen_join`: `SpawnScreenStuff::setVisible(1)` via `0x6cce20`), `0x004b4045` (`network_error_debug` / GameEvent deserialize site). [netcode.md §3](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| P-1 | The client predicts its own player each tick **from the same `PlayerAction` it registers for the wire**; `registerPlayerAction` `0x004904f0` is a small cache-and-call stub, not a re-pack | **verified** (loop level); the exact register→wire send path stays **open** (NEEDS-MORE, cosmetic) | Per-player loop `0x004b8d70` (live): front pending input → set `0x00483e70` → get `0x004913a0` → `handleInput` vtable+0x30 → `performHandleUpdate` `0x004b7680` → `0x004904f0` → pop. `0x004904f0`'s live body: cache `FUN_00484820(p)+0x14` into `param_1+0x1c8`, call `FUN_004b7200`. [netcode.md §4](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| P-2 | Remote players are stepped by the client's own sim **at zero input**, then overwritten by server ghost state every **0.1 s**; **there is no interpolation buffer** | **verified** (the no-buffer claim is read-backed 2026-09-20); the apply step `0x00498ce0` stays **open** | `GameClient::simulatePlayersUpdate` `0x004b6c20` calls `0x004b6a30` (which zeroes input on an empty buffer) per non-local player. Ghost: server `GhostManager::update` `0x08141c70`; client twin `0x0048a130` (read live — UNREAD at research time; gated on `GameClient+0x1d8`) → state-applier `0x00489ed0` → per-entity distance/velocity factor `0x00489cb0` → **`0x00498ce0` unread**. The path holds no snapshot-history/sample repository — single current state with a computed factor. Cadence: `conn+0x18 = 0x3dcccccd` at server `0x08139420` and client `0x0048c520` (0.1f at `0x8c53cc`). [netcode.md §4](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| R-1 | Default per-connection send rate **20 Hz, minimum 10**; the choke lowers by 5 to the floor of 10 while **Σ(rate × 1044) > cap** (`GameServer+0x46c`) | **verified** | `chokeBandwidth` `0x08132cc0` / `setBandwidthChokeLimit` `0x08132c90` / `getBandwidthChokeLimit` `0x08132cb0`; the rate is applied as period `1/rate` in `processGameStateAndSendPackets` `0x08139420` (client twin `0x0048c520`) and written via `changeUpdateFrequency` `0x08132ff0` → `conn+0xc`. [netcode.md §5](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| R-2 | The packet carries a **4-bit action count (≤ 15)**; `GameServer::receive` is the receive path; `setPacketSize` is an **empty stub** | **verified** | `processReceivedPacket` `0x081484e0` reads `readUnsigned(stream, 4)`, each counted entry `readFromStream`. `GameServer::receive` `0x081378d0` (NetServer read into the per-connection BitStream at `conn+0x90`, `setMode(read)`). `setPacketSize` `0x08132c80` = `return;`. [netcode.md §5](subsystems/netcode.md) |
| R-2b | "The client batches ≤ 3 actions per packet" | **open** — the ≤ 3 cap exists only **server-side** in `PlayerActionManager::add` `0x08148120` (`if (3 < count) count = 3`, ring memmove), and that class is reachable only indirectly (one pointer hit → a vtable/interface slot) and server-managed; the client PE has no `PlayerActionManager` and the client send loop was not re-derivable. Do not record "≤ 3" as client-verified wire behaviour | R-2 verdict (NEEDS-MORE). [netcode.md §5–§6](subsystems/netcode.md) |
